Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any symplectic module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 285. The projective resolution has length at most 994 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to symplectic theory, we decompose the problem into 285 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 994. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
